the description auto-optimization loop for a skill.\r\n\r\nPort from Anthropic's official anthropics/skills skill-creator\r\n(run_loop.py), kept client-agnostic:\r\n\r\n  - Stratified 60/40 train/validation split (by should_trigger).\r\n  - Each iteration evaluates the current description (heuristic mode by\r\n    default) and requests an improved description via an LLM improve prompt.\r\n    --improve-mode manual prints the prompt and reads a human/pasted reply;\r\n    --improve-mode cli shells out to a client CLI (default `claude -p`).\r\n  - Validation selects candidates; a separate --final-eval-set is evaluated once\r\n    after selection, without feeding its results into the improvement loop.\r\n  - The improve prompt injects the best-so-far description (highest development\r\n    validation score in the run) as a few-shot precedent, so each round\r\n    improves on validated wording instead of inventing from scratch.\r\n\r\nUsage:\r\n    python scripts/skill_optimize.py --eval-set <evals.json> --skill-dir <skill>         [--holdout 0.4] [--max-iterations 5] [--improve-mode manual|cli]         [--client claude] [--seed 42] [--report <out.json>]\r\n\r\nHeuristic mode is a lexical proxy. Use --eval-mode cli or a separate final CLI\r\nevaluation for client evidence. Exit 1 on improvement/evaluation errors or a\r\nfailed final test; reports are still written. a skill directory for a target LLM client (claude/opencode/codex/deepseek).\r\n\r\nPart of the skill-creator skill (see SKILL.md stage 9). Packaging = adapt the\r\nSKILL.md frontmatter to the client's schema, copy the whole skill tree (minus\r\ncaches), and run a per-client post-check so a package that would not load is\r\nnever emitted (transform + post-check discipline).\r\n\r\nFrontmatter handling (the skill-side twist):\r\n  - `allowed-tools` is the repo's canonical least-privilege whitelist (Claude tool\r\n    names, e.g. `[Read, Grep, Glob, Bash]`):\r\n      * claude keeps the native `allowed-tools` key, normalized to a\r\n        comma-separated string (unknown names kept as-is).\r\n      * opencode translates each Claude name back to an opencode tool-class key\r\n        and merges it into a per-tool `permission` map (see below).\r\n      * codex/deepseek pass it through unchanged (best-effort).\r\n  - A legacy `tools` list whose entries are client labels (`claude`/`opencode`/...)\r\n    is the repo's *supported-clients* metadata, NOT a tool whitelist. It is dropped\r\n    for claude/opencode and left as-is for codex/deepseek. (Retained for backward\r\n    compatibility; the repo no longer emits `tools` in skills.)\r\n  - A legacy `tools` list of actual tool names IS a least-privilege whitelist\r\n    (same treatment as `allowed-tools`):\r\n      * opencode: the whitelist is merged into a per-tool `permission` map\r\n        (whitelisted tool -> allow, other tool-class keys -> deny; an explicit\r\n        `permission` entry always wins). A bare `permission` **string shorthand**\r\n        is NOT kept as a global rule (that would widen/loosen) — the whitelist is\r\n        materialized into per-tool rules instead.\r\n      * claude: the whitelist becomes a comma-separated Claude tool-name string.\r\n  - `allowed-tools` and a legacy `tools` whitelist are unioned when both appear.\r\n  - codex/deepseek: no official skill frontmatter convention — YAML sanity check\r\n    plus a generic name/description check, body passes through.\r\n\r\nUsage:\r\n    python scripts/skill_package.py <skill_dir> --client claude [--client opencode ...]         --out <dir> [--zip]\r\n\r\nOutput layout: <out>/<client>/<skill-name>/  (plus <out>/<client>/<skill-name>.zip\r\nwith --zip). Exit code 0 = all requested clients packaged; 1 = a package failed\r\n(nothing partial for that client); 2 = bad arguments.\r\n�\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000N)\u0001�\u0004Pathz\u001f^---\\s*\\n(.*?)\\n?---(?:\\s*\\n|$)z\u0018^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$)\u0004�\u0006claude�\bopencode�\u0005codex�\bdeepseek)\u0002r\u0004\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0005\u0000\u0000\u0000>\u0004\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0006\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0004\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0007\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0005\u0000\u0000\u0000)\f�\u0004read�\u0004edit�\u0004glob�\u0004grep�\u0004list�\u0004bash�\u0004task�\bwebfetch�\twebsearch�\ttodowrite�\bquestion�\u0005skillr\t\u0000\u0000\u0000)\u0002�\u0005write�\u0005patch)\u0003�\u0005allow�\u0003ask�\u0004deny�\u0004Read�\u0005Write�\u0004Edit�\u0004Bash�\u0004Glob�\u0004Grep�\bWebFetch�\tWebSearch�\u0004Task�\tTodoWrite)\r\nr\b\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0014\u0000\u0000\u0000r\t\u0000\u0000\u0000r\r\n\u0000\u0000\u0000r\r\n\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u000b\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u000f\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0010\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u000e\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0011\u0000\u0000\u0000r\b\u0000\u0000\u0000r\r\n\u0000\u0000\u0000r\r\n\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u000b\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u000f\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0010\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u000e\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0011\u0000\u0000\u0000r\f\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0013\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0012\u0000\u0000\u0000)\r\nr\b\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0014\u0000\u0000\u0000r\t\u0000\u0000\u0000r\r\n\u0000\u0000\u0000r\r\n\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u000b\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u000f\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0010\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u000e\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0011\u0000\u0000\u0000r\f\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0013\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u0012\u0000\u0000\u0000>\u0005\u0000\u0000\u0000�\u0004.git�\u000b.mypy_cache�\u000b.ruff_cache�\r\n.pytest_cache�\u000b__pycache__c\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0001\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000�\u0012\u0000\u0000\u0000�\u0000e\u0000Z\u0001d\u0000Z\u0002d\u0001Z\u0003d\u0002S\u0000)\u0003�\fPackageErrorz;Raised d\u0011e\u001dd\fe!e\u001d\u0019\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000f\u0004d\u0012�\u0004Z\"d\u0013e\u001dd\u0014e\u001dd\u0015e\u001dd\u0011e\u001dd\u0002z\u0007\u0000\u0000d\fe!e\u001d\u0019\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000f\r\nd\u0016�\u0004Z#d\u0017e\u000fd\fe$f\u0004d\u0018�\u0004Z%d\u0019e\u000fd\u001ae\u000fd\fd\u0002f\u0006d\u001b�\u0004Z&d\u001ce\u000fd\u001de\u000fd\u001ee'e\u001d\u0019\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000d\u001fe\u000fd\fe(f\r\nd �\u0004Z)d\fe\u001ff\u0002d!�\u0004Z*e+d\"k\u0002\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000r\u001a\u0002\u0000e\tj,\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0002\u0000e*�\u0000\u0000\u0000�\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000�\u0001\u0000\u0000�\u0001\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0001\u0000d\u0002S\u0000d\u0002S\u0000)$u�\u0006\u0000\u0000Run one eval scenario via a client CLI and record a benchmark-ready run directory.\r\n\r\nCloses the gap in the quantitative loop: `skill_benchmark.py` can read a\r\nworkspace layout, but nothing produced one. This executor runs a real task\r\nprompt with the skill available (`--skill-dir`) or without it, and writes the\r\nper-run artifacts the grader / aggregator expect:\r\n\r\n    <run-dir>/\r\n    ├── transcript.md      # prompt + raw client output\r\n    ├── outputs/\r\n    │   ├── response.txt   # extracted assistant text\r\n    │   └── artifacts/     # generated files with workspace-relative paths\r\n    ├── artifacts.json     # retained files, hashes, exclusions, collection errors\r\n    ├── metrics.json       # execution status, usage when available, provenance\r\n    └── timing.json        # total_duration_seconds\r\n\r\nmetrics.json lives in the run-dir root (sibling of timing.json / grading.json).\r\nThe grader merges it into grading.json execution_metrics, and\r\nskill_benchmark.py reads it directly as a fallback.\r\n\r\nThe skill (when given) is copied into a throwaway client workspace, so the\r\n\"without_skill\" run never sees it. `--client-cmd` overrides the invoked command\r\nfor clients whose CLI differs or for testing; `{prompt}` is substituted.\r\nInputs are copied with --input-dir into inputs/; --output-dir names a relative\r\nworkspace directory (default outputs/). Generated cwd files are retained too.\r\nRun directories must be empty, so prior grading cannot leak into another run.\r\n\r\nUsage:\r\n    python scripts/skill_scenario.py --client opencode --prompt \"<task>\"         --run-dir <workspace>/iteration-1/eval-x/with_skill/run-1         [--skill-dir <skill>] [--model M] [--timeout 300] [--keep]\r\n\r\nExit code 0 = run recorded; 1 = error.\r\n�\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000N)\u0002�\bdatetime�\btimezone)\u0001�\u0004Path)\u0004�\u000eassistant_text�\u000fexecution_error�\u000estream_metrics�\r\ntool_calls)\u0001�\r\nrun_clientz\u0010.opencode/skillsc\u0002\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0003\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0003\u0000\u0000\u0000�$\u0000\u0000\u0000�\u0000g\u0000d\u0001�\u0001|\u0001r\u0004d\u0002|\u0001g\u0002n\u0001g\u0000z\u0000\u0000\u0000|\u0000g\u0001z\u0000\u0000\u0000S\u0000)\u0003N)\u0004�\bopencode�\u0003runz\b--format�\u0004jsonz\u0002-m�\u0000�\u0002�\u0006prompt�\u0005models\u0002\u0000\u0000\u0000 assistant text from opencode or Claude; plain overrides still work.)\u0001r\u0006\u0000\u0000\u0000�\u0001r*\u0000\u0000\u0000s\u0001\u0000\u0000\u0000 r\u0013\u0000\u0000\u0000�\fextract_textr-\u0000\u0000\u0000U\u0000\u0000\u0000s\u0010\u0000\u0000\u0000�\u0000�\u000b\u0019�#�\u000b\u001e�\u000b\u001e�\u0004\u001er\u0015\u0000\u0000\u0000c\u0001\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0005\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0003\u0000\u0000\u0000�:\u0000\u0000\u0000�\u0000t\u0001\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000t\u0003\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000|\u0000�\u0001\u0000\u0000�\u0001\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000�\u0001\u0000\u0000�\u0001\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000\u0000S\u0000)\u0001a\u0019\u0002\u0000\u0000Count tool invocations in a client's JSON event stream.\r\n\r\n    Parses each JSON line instead of substring-counting a serialized key, so the\r\n    count is independent of the client's whitespace/formatting. opencode emits\r\n    tool events as `{\"type\":\"tool_use\",\"part\":{\"type\":\"tool\",...}}`; accept both\r\n    that and a top-level `type == \"tool\"` (older/simplified streams). Claude\r\n    partial/final events are deduplicated. utilities for skill-creator scripts.\r\n\r\nTwo groups of helpers live here so the CLI scripts never drift apart:\r\n\r\n1. **Section-header patterns** (`WHEN_TO_USE_PATTERNS` / `EXAMPLES_PATTERNS` /\r\n   `LIMITATIONS_PATTERNS` + `has_*_section`) — the single source of truth used by\r\n   both `skill_validate.py` and `skill_compare.py`.\r\n2. **Frontmatter / trigger helpers** — `parse_frontmatter` (PyYAML, imported\r\n   lazily so stdlib-only callers such as `skill_eval.py` stay dependency-free),\r\n   `parse_skill_md`, `eval_query`, and the CJK-aware `keyword_tokens` / `classify`\r\n   heuristic that `skill_eval.py` reuses.\r\n\r\nPort of the parse utility from Anthropic's official anthropics/skills\r\nskill-creator, generalized for the four-client SKILL.md format used by this\r\nrepository (frontmatter keys name/description are common utilities for skill-creator scripts.\r\n\r\nTwo groups of helpers live here so the CLI scripts never drift apart:\r\n\r\n1. **Section-header patterns** (`WHEN_TO_USE_PATTERNS` / `EXAMPLES_PATTERNS` /\r\n   `LIMITATIONS_PATTERNS` + `has_*_section`) — the single source of truth used by\r\n   both `skill_validate.py` and `skill_compare.py`.\r\n2. **Frontmatter / trigger helpers** — `parse_frontmatter` (PyYAML, imported\r\n   lazily so stdlib-only callers such as `skill_eval.py` stay dependency-free),\r\n   `parse_skill_md`, `eval_query`, and the CJK-aware `keyword_tokens` / `classify`\r\n   heuristic that `skill_eval.py` reuses.\r\n\r\nPort of the parse utility from Anthropic's official anthropics/skills\r\nskill-creator, generalized for the four-client SKILL.md format used by this\r\nrepository (frontmatter keys name/description are common tool invocations in a client's JSON event stream.\r\n\r\n    Parses each JSON line instead of substring-counting a serialized key, so the\r\n    count is independent of the client's whitespace/formatting. opencode emits\r\n    tool events as `{\"type\":\"tool_use\",\"part\":{\"type\":\"tool\",...}}`; accept both\r\n    that and a top-level `type == \"tool\"` (older/simplified streams). Claude\r\n    partial/final events are deduplicated. utilities for skill-creator scripts.\r\n\r\nTwo groups of helpers live here so the CLI scripts never drift apart:\r\n\r\n1. **Section-header patterns** (`WHEN_TO_USE_PATTERNS` / `EXAMPLES_PATTERNS` /\r\n   `LIMITATIONS_PATTERNS` + `has_*_section`) — the single source of truth used by\r\n   both `skill_validate.py` and `skill_compare.py`.\r\n2. **Frontmatter / trigger helpers** — `parse_frontmatter` (PyYAML, imported\r\n   lazily so stdlib-only callers such as `skill_eval.py` stay dependency-free),\r\n   `parse_skill_md`, `eval_query`, and the CJK-aware `keyword_tokens` / `classify`\r\n   heuristic that `skill_eval.py` reuses.\r\n\r\nPort of the parse utility from Anthropic's official anthropics/skills\r\nskill-creator, generalized for the four-client SKILL.md format used by this\r\nrepository (frontmatter keys name/description are common utilities for skill-creator scripts.\r\n\r\nTwo groups of helpers live here so the CLI scripts never drift apart:\r\n\r\n1. **Section-header patterns** (`WHEN_TO_USE_PATTERNS` / `EXAMPLES_PATTERNS` /\r\n   `LIMITATIONS_PATTERNS` + `has_*_section`) — the single source of truth used by\r\n   both `skill_validate.py` and `skill_compare.py`.\r\n2. **Frontmatter / trigger helpers** — `parse_frontmatter` (PyYAML, imported\r\n   lazily so stdlib-only callers such as `skill_eval.py` stay dependency-free),\r\n   `parse_skill_md`, `eval_query`, and the CJK-aware `keyword_tokens` / `classify`\r\n   heuristic that `skill_eval.py` reuses.\r\n\r\nPort of the parse utility from Anthropic's official anthropics/skills\r\nskill-creator, generalized for the four-client SKILL.md format used by this\r\nrepository (frontmatter keys name/description are common # 评测与基准 JSON Schema（benchmark-schema）\n2: \n3: > 来源：移植自 Anthropic `anthropics/skills/skills/skill-creator/references/schemas.md`，字段名与本地 skill-creator 的 `skill_eval.py` / `skill_optimize.py` / `skill_benchmark.py` 对齐。客户端无关（claude/opencode/codex/deepseek 通用）。\n4: \n5: ## 目录\n6: \n7: - 触发评测输入与 evaluation 结果\n8: - skill_optimize 开发验证与最终测试\n9: - grading / analyzer / timing / metrics 产物\n10: - benchmark 汇总与工作区布局\n11: \n12: ## evals.json（触发评测集）\n13: \n14: 技能目录 `evals/evals.json`（或直接挂在技能目录下的 `evals.json`）：\n15: \n16: ```json\n17: {\n18:   \"skill_name\": \"example-skill\",\n19:   \"evals\": [\n20:     {\n21:       \"id\": 1,\n22:       \"query\": \"用户会真实说的话（应触发该技能）\",\n23:       \"should_trigger\": true,\n24:       \"expected_output\": \"预期结果描述\"\n25:     },\n26:     {\n27:       \"id\": 2,\n28:       \"query\": \"近似干扰项（关键词重叠但应不触发）\",\n29:       \"should_trigger\": false,\n30:       \"expected_output\": \"\"\n31:     }\n32:   ]\n33: }\n34: ```\n35: \n36: 字段：\n37: - `skill_name`：与 SKILL.md frontmatter 的 `name` 一致\n38: - `evals[].id`：唯一整数\n39: - `evals[].query`：真实用户提示词（不是抽象请求）\n40: - `evals[].should_trigger`：是否应触发本技能\n41: - `evals[].expected_output`：成功时的人类可读描述（可选）\n42: \n43: ## evaluation 结果（skill_eval 输出）\n44: \n45: `skill_eval.py --json` 输出，同时含每条查询与汇总：\n46: \n47: ```json\n48: {\n49:   \"skill_name\": \"example-skill\",\n50:   \"description\": \"当前 description\",\n51:   \"mode\": \"heuristic\",\n52:   \"results\": [\n53:     {\n54:       \"query\": \"...\",\n55:       \"should_trigger\": true,\n56:       \"triggered\": true,\n57:       \"pass\": true\n58:     }\n59:   ],\n60:   \"summary\": {\n61:     \"passed\": 8, \"failed\": 2, \"errors\": 1, \"total\": 10,\n62:     \"attempted\": 11, \"coverage\": 0.909,\n63:     \"precision\": 0.9, \"recall\": 0.8\n64:   }\n65: }\n66: ```\n67: \n68: - `total` 仅计有效评测查询，`attempted` 包含运行错误；`coverage=total/attempted`，无查询为 0。\n69: - `precision` / `recall` 无分母时为 null（文本 N/A）；全部运行错误不能解释为 100% 准确。\n70: - 顶层另记录 `client` / `model`（heuristic 为 null）、`threshold`、`runs_per_query`，便于重现。\n71: - 默认仅出报告，退出码 0；`--fail-on-error` 在 errors>0 时退出 1，`--fail-on-mismatch` 在 failed>0 时退出 1，组合使用可作质量门。报告先写出再返回失败码。\n72: \n73: ## skill_optimize 迭代记录（description 自动优化）\n74: \n75: `skill_optimize.py --report` 输出为最终 JSON；`history[]` 记录开发 train/validation 得分。独立最终查询集只在选优后运行一次：\n76: \n77: ```json\n78: {\n79:   \"schema_version\": 2,\n80:   \"skill_name\": \"example-skill\",\n81:   \"original_description\": \"...\",\n82:   \"best_description\": \"...\",\n83:   \"best_score\": \"4/4\",\n84:   \"selection_set\": \"validation\",\n85:   \"evaluation_mode\": \"heuristic\",\n86:   \"confirmation\": \"not_run\",\n87:   \"final_evaluation\": null,\n88:   \"iterations_run\": 3,\n89:   \"exit_reason\": \"train_all_passed (iteration 3)\",\n90:   \"holdout\": 0.4,\n91:   \"history\": [\n92:     {\n93:       \"iteration\": 1,\n94:       \"description\": \"...\",\n95:       \"train_passed\": 6, \"train_total\": 6,\n96:       \"train_errors\": 0,\n97:       \"validation_passed\": 4, \"validation_total\": 4, \"validation_errors\": 0,\n98:       \"validation_results\": [],\n99:       \"test_passed\": 4, \"test_total\": 4,\n100:       \"train_results\": []\n101:     }\n102:   ]\n103: }\n104: ```\n105: \n106: - `best_description` 按开发 validation 通过率选取；该集参与反复选择，不能充当独立测试。开发集按 should_trigger 分层 60/40 切分（`--holdout 0.4`）。样本过少或 holdout=0 时退回 train，`selection_set` 显式标记；开发查询重复会被拒绝。\n107: - `history[].test_passed/test_total` 保留为 validation 的兼容别名；顶层 `legacy_test_fields` 说明其含义。`seed`、`client`、`model`、`threshold`、`runs_per_query` 记录运行参数。\n108: - `--eval-mode heuristic|cli` 决定开发评分方式；`--improve-mode` 只决定怎样生成候选。CLI 会评测写入临时安装副本的候选描述。\n109: - `--final-eval-set` 必须与开发集查询不重叠；最后只评测选定描述一次。`final_evaluation` 含 mode/results/summary，summary 与 skill_eval 一致；默认 `--final-mode cli`，未传最终集则为 null。\n110: - `confirmation`：not_run（未测）、proxy_only（词面通过）、dispatch_passed（opencode 派发通过）、structured_passed（Claude 结构化调用/读取信号通过）、failed（触发不符）、error（运行/改写失败）。旧 approximate_passed 仅属于历史全文匹配报告。最终查询与结果不进入改写提示或候选选择；最终集一经用于指导后续调整，需换新集。\n111: - 优化/评测错误或最终测试不通过返回 1，仍保留报告。缺少最终测试或只测 heuristic 不能宣称已验证真实触发。\n112: \n113: ## grading.json（单次运行评分，供 skill_benchmark 读取）\n114: \n115: 每个运行目录 `<workspace>/iteration-N/eval-<name>/<config>/run-1/grading.json`：\n116: \n117: ```json\n118: {\n119:   \"expectations\": [\n120:     { \"text\": \"输出包含 X\", \"passed\": true, \"evidence\": \"...\" }\n121:   ],\n122:   \"summary\": { \"passed\": 1, \"failed\": 0, \"total\": 1, \"pass_rate\": 1.0 },\n123:   \"execution_metrics\": { \"total_tool_calls\": 15, \"errors_encountered\": 0, \"output_chars\": 12450 },\n124:   \"timing\": { \"total_duration_seconds\": 191.0 },\n125:   \"user_notes_summary\": { \"uncertainties\": [], \"needs_review\": [], \"workarounds\": [] }\n126: }\n127: ```\n128: \n129: > 注意：`expectations[].text` / `.passed` / `.evidence` 三个字段名是视图与汇总的约定，不要改名为其他写法。\n130: >\n131: > `grading.json` 由**评分子代理**产出（`agents/skill_grader.md`，拉起或内联执行均可）；完整字段（claims / user_notes_summary / eval_feedback 等）见该文件。\n132: \n133: ## analyzer 笔记（skill_benchmark --notes 输入）\n134: \n135: 分析子代理（`agents/skill_analyzer.md` 模式二）产出的观察笔记为 **JSON 字符串数组**，经脚本合并进 `benchmark.json` 的 `notes`：\n136: \n137: ```bash\n138: python scripts/skill_benchmark.py <workspace>/iteration-N --skill-name <名> --notes <notes文件>\n139: ```\n140: \n141: ## timing.json（可选，运行计时）\n142: \n143: `<run-dir>/timing.json`，由 `skill_scenario.py` 产出。**必写**：`total_duration_seconds`（墙钟秒）与 `timed_out`（是否超时）；若客户端回报了 token 数与精确毫秒，再补 `total_tokens` / `duration_ms`（可选）：\n144: \n145: ```json\n146: {\n147:   \"total_duration_seconds\": 23.3,\n148:   \"timed_out\": false,\n149:   \"total_tokens\": 84852,\n150:   \"duration_ms\": 23332\n151: }\n152: ```\n153: \n154: ## metrics.json（可选，场景执行计数）\n155: \n156: `<run-dir>/metrics.json`，由 `skill_scenario.py` 产出（与 `timing.json`/`grading.json` 同级；`outputs/` 是其子目录）：\n157: \n158: ```json\n159: {\n160:   \"client\": \"opencode\",\n161:   \"model\": \"\",\n162:   \"skill\": \"example-skill\",\n163:   \"returncode\": 0,\n164:   \"output_chars\": 12450,\n165:   \"total_tool_calls\": 15\n166: }\n167: ```\n168: \n169: - **读取契约**：评分子代理从 `{outputs_dir}/../metrics.json`（即 run 根目录）并入 `grading.json` 的 `execution_metrics`。汇总器优先读 run 根原始 metrics/timing，再回退评分内嵌字段；原始记录的真实 0 不能被回退值覆盖。metrics/timing 可省略以兼容外部执行器；提供后必须为有效 JSON 对象，执行失败、超时不能被评分结果覆盖。\n170: \n171: ## benchmark.json / benchmark.md（skill_benchmark 产物，schema_version=2）\n172: \n173: 由 `skill_benchmark.py <workspace>/iteration-N` 生成。默认报告模式仍退出 0；`--strict` 写完报告后，在存在 error/incomplete 或无完整配对通过率时退出 1。未知成本本身不使 strict 失败。\n174: \n175: ```json\n176: {\n177:   \"schema_version\": 2,\n178:   \"metadata\": {\n179:     \"skill_name\": \"example-skill\", \"skill_path\": null,\n180:     \"executor_model\": null, \"executor_models\": [], \"clients\": [\"opencode\"],\n181:     \"unknown_model_runs\": 2, \"unknown_client_runs\": 0,\n182:     \"timestamp\": \"2026-09-29T00:00:00Z\", \"evals_run\": [1],\n183:     \"runs_per_configuration\": 1,\n184:     \"primary_configuration\": \"with_skill\", \"baseline_configuration\": \"without_skill\"\n185:   },\n186:   \"runs\": [{\n187:     \"eval_id\": 1, \"configuration\": \"with_skill\", \"run_number\": 1,\n188:     \"run_path\": \"eval-example/with_skill/run-1\", \"status\": \"completed\", \"issues\": [],\n189:     \"execution_status\": \"completed\", \"client\": \"opencode\", \"model\": null, \"models\": [],\n190:     \"model_source\": null, \"requested_model\": null, \"tokens_source\": null, \"tool_calls_source\": null,\n191:     \"measurement_sources\": {\"tokens\": null, \"time_seconds\": \"timing.json.total_duration_seconds\", \"tool_calls\": null},\n192:     \"result\": {\"pass_rate\": 1.0, \"passed\": 1, \"failed\": 0, \"total\": 1,\n193:                \"time_seconds\": 42.5, \"tokens\": null, \"tool_calls\": null},\n194:     \"expectations\": [{\"text\": \"正确完成\", \"passed\": true, \"evidence\": \"产物核验\"}], \"notes\": []\n195:   }],\n196:   \"run_summary\": {\n197:     \"with_skill\": {\n198:       \"runs\": 1, \"attempted\": 1, \"completed\": 1, \"errors\": 0, \"incomplete\": 0, \"coverage\": 1.0,\n199:       \"pass_rate\": {\"n\": 1, \"mean\": 1.0, \"stddev\": 0.0, \"min\": 1.0, \"max\": 1.0},\n200:       \"time_seconds\": {\"n\": 1, \"mean\": 42.5, \"stddev\": 0.0, \"min\": 42.5, \"max\": 42.5},\n201:       \"tokens\": {\"n\": 0, \"mean\": null, \"stddev\": null, \"min\": null, \"max\": null}\n202:     },\n203:     \"without_skill\": {\"...\": \"同上结构；runs[] 中也有对应记录，此处省略\"},\n204:     \"delta\": {\"primary\": \"with_skill\", \"baseline\": \"without_skill\",\n205:               \"expected_pairs\": 1, \"paired_samples\": 1, \"coverage\": 1.0,\n206:               \"duplicate_keys\": [], \"identity_mismatches\": [],\n207:               \"metric_pairs\": {\"pass_rate\": 1, \"time_seconds\": 1, \"tokens\": 0},\n208:               \"pass_rate\": \"+1.00\", \"time_seconds\": \"+13.0\", \"tokens\": null,\n209:               \"note\": \"cost delta unavailable where any paired measurement is missing\"}\n210:   },\n211:   \"notes\": []\n212: }\n213: ```\n214: \n215: ### 尝试、有效评分与缺失值\n216: \n217: - `runs[]` 记录所有发现的 run 目录，即使缺少或损坏 grading 也保留。`runs` 是 attempted 的兼容别名；`completed` 为有效评分且无已知执行失败的次数，errors 为明确失败/超时，incomplete 为评分缺失/损坏、指标文件无效或数据矛盾；三者之和等于 attempted。coverage=completed/attempted，无尝试为 0。\n218: - `status` 为 completed/error/incomplete，`issues[]` 保留原因码与文件/字段。执行失败优先于评分，失败运行即使得到 100% 的 grading 也不进入通过率。`execution_status=unknown` 表示外部执行器未提供退出状态，不能宣称已验证执行成功。\n219: - summary 的 passed/failed/total 必须是非负整数、total>0、passed+failed=total。缺省 pass_rate 从计数推导；提供时必须为 [0,1] 有限数且与比值一致（允许两位小数舍入）。非空 expectations 必须是完整列表，布尔 passed 的计数与 summary 一致；summary-only 旧记录可读。\n220: - tokens/time/tool_calls 未知为 null，不能用 output_chars 估算 tokens。数值必须有限且非负；真实 0 保留。每项统计记录有效 n，n=0 的均值、标准差、最小、最大均为 null；Markdown 显示 N/A。\n221: - 通过率仅统计 completed；成本统计所有尝试中已知的观测值，含失败尝试，避免隐藏失败开销。`measurement_sources` 指明原始字段。\n222: - model/client 来自 metrics（缺省回退内嵌 execution_metrics）。保留 models/model_source/requested_model/tokens_source/tool_calls_source，区分客户端观测与请求参数。非空观测 models 集合优先于单个 model；多个已知模型时 model=null，不用 requested_model 伪装为单模型。空集合表示未记录的默认模型，保留未知。metadata 汇总模型/客户端集合及未知次数；仅当全部尝试明确记录同一模型时设置 executor_model，其他情况为 null。\n223: \n224: ### 配对与增益边界\n225: \n226: - configuration 默认为 with_skill/without_skill；`--primary`/`--baseline` 显式指定角色，也识别 skill/treatment、baseline/control/no_skill 别名。未知角色不自动拿无关目录作对照。\n227: - 配对键为 **eval_id + run_number**。eval_id 优先来自场景的 eval_metadata.json，缺省用 eval 目录名；不同场景或不同重复编号不能配对。同一配置重复键标记歧义，不任选一条。\n228: - expected_pairs 是两配置已观察键的并集大小；paired_samples 只计两侧各有唯一 completed 记录且无已知 models 集合/client 差异的配对；coverage 为其比例。这里只覆盖已发现目录，**无法证明未创建目录的计划场景也已运行**。\n229: - 仅所有已观察键完整配对时输出通过率差值；缺失、失败、重号或已知模型/客户端不一致时 delta=null，并给 note，避免幸存样本制造增益。未记录模型/客户端的旧数据仍可描述性比较，但身份一致性未经确认。\n230: - 成本差值另要求所有配对都提供该指标；否则仅该项 delta=null。差值方向为 primary−baseline，沿用原字符串格式。均值与标准差是描述统计；一次配对不证明显著性或因果关系。\n231: - benchmark.md 展示每项 n、各配置 attempted/completed/errors/incomplete/coverage 和配对覆盖。分析时先检查完整性与模型身份，再解释差值及方差。\n232: \n233: ## 工作区布局（约定）\n234: \n235: ```\n236: <workspace>/iteration-N/\n237: └── eval-<descriptive-name>/\n238:     ├── with_skill/\n239:     │   └── run-1/\n240:     │       ├── transcript.md      # skill_scenario.py\n241:     │       ├── metrics.json       # skill_scenario.py（run 根，见上）\n242:     │       ├── timing.json\n243:     │       ├── outputs/\n244:     │       │   └── response.txt   # skill_scenario.py\n245:     │       └── grading.json       # 评分子代理\n246:     └── without_skill/\n247:         └── run-1/\n248:             └── ...                # 同结构\n249: ```\n250: \n251: 对比顺序：先检查尝试覆盖、配对完整性与模型/客户端身份，再解释可用的通过率和成本差值。高方差提示需要更多重复运行；不要仅凭标准差把原因定为 flaky。\n\n(End of file - total 251 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"# 评测与基准 JSON Schema（benchmark-schema）\n\n> 来源：移植自 Anthropic `anthropics/skills/skills/skill-creator/references/schemas.md`，字段名与本地 skill-creator 的 `skill_eval.py` / `skill_optimize.py` / `skill_benchmark.py` 对齐。客户端无关（claude/opencode/codex/deepseek 通用）。\n\n## 目录\n\n- 触发评测输入与 evaluation 结果\n- skill_optimize 开发验证与最终测试\n- grading / analyzer / timing / metrics 产物\n- benchmark 汇总与工作区布局\n\n## evals.json（触发评测集）\n\n技能目录 `evals/evals.json`（或直接挂在技能目录下的 `evals.json`）：\n\n```json\n{\n  \"skill_name\": \"example-skill\",\n  \"evals\": [\n    {","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"C:\\Users\\Administrator\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\scenario-vtnlmomu\\.opencode\\skills\\skill-creator\\references\\benchmark-schema.md","text":"# 评测与基准 JSON Schema（benchmark-schema）\n\n> 来源：移植自 Anthropic `anthropics/skills/skills/skill-creator/references/schemas.md`，字段名与本地 skill-creator 的 `skill_eval.py` / `skill_optimize.py` / `skill_benchmark.py` 对齐。客户端无关（claude/opencode/codex/deepseek 通用）。\n\n## 目录\n\n- 触发评测输入与 evaluation 结果\n- skill_optimize 开发验证与最终测试\n- grading / analyzer / timing / metrics 产物\n- benchmark 汇总与工作区布局\n\n## evals.json（触发评测集）\n\n技能目录 `evals/evals.json`（或直接挂在技能目录下的 `evals.json`）：\n\n```json\n{\n  \"skill_name\": \"example-skill\",\n  \"evals\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": 1,\n      \"query\": \"用户会真实说的话（应触发该技能）\",\n      \"should_trigger\": true,\n      \"expected_output\": \"预期结果描述\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": 2,\n      \"query\": \"近似干扰项（关键词重叠但应不触发）\",\n      \"should_trigger\": false,\n      \"expected_output\": \"\"\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n字段：\n- `skill_name`：与 SKILL.md frontmatter 的 `name` 一致\n- `evals[].id`：唯一整数\n- `evals[].query`：真实用户提示词（不是抽象请求）\n- `evals[].should_trigger`：是否应触发本技能\n- `evals[].expected_output`：成功时的人类可读描述（可选）\n\n## evaluation 结果（skill_eval 输出）\n\n`skill_eval.py --json` 输出，同时含每条查询与汇总：\n\n```json\n{\n  \"skill_name\": \"example-skill\",\n  \"description\": \"当前 description\",\n  \"mode\": \"heuristic\",\n  \"results\": [\n    {\n      \"query\": \"...\",\n      \"should_trigger\": true,\n      \"triggered\": true,\n      \"pass\": true\n    }\n  ],\n  \"summary\": {\n    \"passed\": 8, \"failed\": 2, \"errors\": 1, \"total\": 10,\n    \"attempted\": 11, \"coverage\": 0.909,\n    \"precision\": 0.9, \"recall\": 0.8\n  }\n}\n```\n\n- `total` 仅计有效评测查询，`attempted` 包含运行错误；`coverage=total/attempted`，无查询为 0。\n- `precision` / `recall` 无分母时为 null（文本 N/A）；全部运行错误不能解释为 100% 准确。\n- 顶层另记录 `client` / `model`（heuristic 为 null）、`threshold`、`runs_per_query`，便于重现。\n- 默认仅出报告，退出码 0；`--fail-on-error` 在 errors>0 时退出 1，`--fail-on-mismatch` 在 failed>0 时退出 1，组合使用可作质量门。报告先写出再返回失败码。\n\n## skill_optimize 迭代记录（description 自动优化）\n\n`skill_optimize.py --report` 输出为最终 JSON；`history[]` 记录开发 train/validation 得分。独立最终查询集只在选优后运行一次：\n\n```json\n{\n  \"schema_version\": 2,\n  \"skill_name\": \"example-skill\",\n  \"original_description\": \"...\",\n  \"best_description\": \"...\",\n  \"best_score\": \"4/4\",\n  \"selection_set\": \"validation\",\n  \"evaluation_mode\": \"heuristic\",\n  \"confirmation\": \"not_run\",\n  \"final_evaluation\": null,\n  \"iterations_run\": 3,\n  \"exit_reason\": \"train_all_passed (iteration 3)\",\n  \"holdout\": 0.4,\n  \"history\": [\n    {\n      \"iteration\": 1,\n      \"description\": \"...\",\n      \"train_passed\": 6, \"train_total\": 6,\n      \"train_errors\": 0,\n      \"validation_passed\": 4, \"validation_total\": 4, \"validation_errors\": 0,\n      \"validation_results\": [],\n      \"test_passed\": 4, \"test_total\": 4,\n      \"train_results\": []\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n- `best_description` 按开发 validation 通过率选取；该集参与反复选择，不能充当独立测试。开发集按 should_trigger 分层 60/40 切分（`--holdout 0.4`）。样本过少或 holdout=0 时退回 train，`selection_set` 显式标记；开发查询重复会被拒绝。\n- `history[].test_passed/test_total` 保留为 validation 的兼容别名；顶层 `legacy_test_fields` 说明其含义。`seed`、`client`、`model`、`threshold`、`runs_per_query` 记录运行参数。\n- `--eval-mode heuristic|cli` 决定开发评分方式；`--improve-mode` 只决定怎样生成候选。CLI 会评测写入临时安装副本的候选描述。\n- `--final-eval-set` 必须与开发集查询不重叠；最后只评测选定描述一次。`final_evaluation` 含 mode/results/summary，summary 与 skill_eval 一致；默认 `--final-mode cli`，未传最终集则为 null。\n- `confirmation`：not_run（未测）、proxy_only（词面通过）、dispatch_passed（opencode 派发通过）、structured_passed（Claude 结构化调用/读取信号通过）、failed（触发不符）、error（运行/改写失败）。旧 approximate_passed 仅属于历史全文匹配报告。最终查询与结果不进入改写提示或候选选择；最终集一经用于指导后续调整，需换新集。\n- 优化/评测错误或最终测试不通过返回 1，仍保留报告。缺少最终测试或只测 heuristic 不能宣称已验证真实触发。\n\n## grading.json（单次运行评分，供 skill_benchmark 读取）\n\n每个运行目录 `<workspace>/iteration-N/eval-<name>/<config>/run-1/grading.json`：\n\n```json\n{\n  \"expectations\": [\n    { \"text\": \"输出包含 X\", \"passed\": true, \"evidence\": \"...\" }\n  ],\n  \"summary\": { \"passed\": 1, \"failed\": 0, \"total\": 1, \"pass_rate\": 1.0 },\n  \"execution_metrics\": { \"total_tool_calls\": 15, \"errors_encountered\": 0, \"output_chars\": 12450 },\n  \"timing\": { \"total_duration_seconds\": 191.0 },\n  \"user_notes_summary\": { \"uncertainties\": [], \"needs_review\": [], \"workarounds\": [] }\n}\n```\n\n> 注意：`expectations[].text` / `.passed` / `.evidence` 三个字段名是视图与汇总的约定，不要改名为其他写法。\n>\n> `grading.json` 由**评分子代理**产出（`agents/skill_grader.md`，拉起或内联执行均可）；完整字段（claims / user_notes_summary / eval_feedback 等）见该文件。\n\n## analyzer 笔记（skill_benchmark --notes 输入）\n\n分析子代理（`agents/skill_analyzer.md` 模式二）产出的观察笔记为 **JSON 字符串数组**，经脚本合并进 `benchmark.json` 的 `notes`：\n\n```bash\npython scripts/skill_benchmark.py <workspace>/iteration-N --skill-name <名> --notes <notes文件>\n```\n\n## timing.json（可选，运行计时）\n\n`<run-dir>/timing.json`，由 `skill_scenario.py` 产出。**必写**：`total_duration_seconds`（墙钟秒）与 `timed_out`（是否超时）；若客户端回报了 token 数与精确毫秒，再补 `total_tokens` / `duration_ms`（可选）：\n\n```json\n{\n  \"total_duration_seconds\": 23.3,\n  \"timed_out\": false,\n  \"total_tokens\": 84852,\n  \"duration_ms\": 23332\n}\n```\n\n## metrics.json（可选，场景执行计数）\n\n`<run-dir>/metrics.json`，由 `skill_scenario.py` 产出（与 `timing.json`/`grading.json` 同级；`outputs/` 是其子目录）：\n\n```json\n{\n  \"client\": \"opencode\",\n  \"model\": \"\",\n  \"skill\": \"example-skill\",\n  \"returncode\": 0,\n  \"output_chars\": 12450,\n  \"total_tool_calls\": 15\n}\n```\n\n- **读取契约**：评分子代理从 `{outputs_dir}/../metrics.json`（即 run 根目录）并入 `grading.json` 的 `execution_metrics`。汇总器优先读 run 根原始 metrics/timing，再回退评分内嵌字段；原始记录的真实 0 不能被回退值覆盖。metrics/timing 可省略以兼容外部执行器；提供后必须为有效 JSON 对象，执行失败、超时不能被评分结果覆盖。\n\n## benchmark.json / benchmark.md（skill_benchmark 产物，schema_version=2）\n\n由 `skill_benchmark.py <workspace>/iteration-N` 生成。默认报告模式仍退出 0；`--strict` 写完报告后，在存在 error/incomplete 或无完整配对通过率时退出 1。未知成本本身不使 strict 失败。\n\n```json\n{\n  \"schema_version\": 2,\n  \"metadata\": {\n    \"skill_name\": \"example-skill\", \"skill_path\": null,\n    \"executor_model\": null, \"executor_models\": [], \"clients\": [\"opencode\"],\n    \"unknown_model_runs\": 2, \"unknown_client_runs\": 0,\n    \"timestamp\": \"2026-09-29T00:00:00Z\", \"evals_run\": [1],\n    \"runs_per_configuration\": 1,\n    \"primary_configuration\": \"with_skill\", \"baseline_configuration\": \"without_skill\"\n  },\n  \"runs\": [{\n    \"eval_id\": 1, \"configuration\": \"with_skill\", \"run_number\": 1,\n    \"run_path\": \"eval-example/with_skill/run-1\", \"status\": \"completed\", \"issues\": [],\n    \"execution_status\": \"completed\", \"client\": \"opencode\", \"model\": null, \"models\": [],\n    \"model_source\": null, \"requested_model\": null, \"tokens_source\": null, \"tool_calls_source\": null,\n    \"measurement_sources\": {\"tokens\": null, \"time_seconds\": \"timing.json.total_duration_seconds\", \"tool_calls\": null},\n    \"result\": {\"pass_rate\": 1.0, \"passed\": 1, \"failed\": 0, \"total\": 1,\n               \"time_seconds\": 42.5, \"tokens\": null, \"tool_calls\": null},\n    \"expectations\": [{\"text\": \"正确完成\", \"passed\": true, \"evidence\": \"产物核验\"}], \"notes\": []\n  }],\n  \"run_summary\": {\n    \"with_skill\": {\n      \"runs\": 1, \"attempted\": 1, \"completed\": 1, \"errors\": 0, \"incomplete\": 0, \"coverage\": 1.0,\n      \"pass_rate\": {\"n\": 1, \"mean\": 1.0, \"stddev\": 0.0, \"min\": 1.0, \"max\": 1.0},\n      \"time_seconds\": {\"n\": 1, \"mean\": 42.5, \"stddev\": 0.0, \"min\": 42.5, \"max\": 42.5},\n      \"tokens\": {\"n\": 0, \"mean\": null, \"stddev\": null, \"min\": null, \"max\": null}\n    },\n    \"without_skill\": {\"...\": \"同上结构；runs[] 中也有对应记录，此处省略\"},\n    \"delta\": {\"primary\": \"with_skill\", \"baseline\": \"without_skill\",\n              \"expected_pairs\": 1, \"paired_samples\": 1, \"coverage\": 1.0,\n              \"duplicate_keys\": [], \"identity_mismatches\": [],\n              \"metric_pairs\": {\"pass_rate\": 1, \"time_seconds\": 1, \"tokens\": 0},\n              \"pass_rate\": \"+1.00\", \"time_seconds\": \"+13.0\", \"tokens\": null,\n              \"note\": \"cost delta unavailable where any paired measurement is missing\"}\n  },\n  \"notes\": []\n}\n```\n\n### 尝试、有效评分与缺失值\n\n- `runs[]` 记录所有发现的 run 目录，即使缺少或损坏 grading 也保留。`runs` 是 attempted 的兼容别名；`completed` 为有效评分且无已知执行失败的次数，errors 为明确失败/超时，incomplete 为评分缺失/损坏、指标文件无效或数据矛盾；三者之和等于 attempted。coverage=completed/attempted，无尝试为 0。\n- `status` 为 completed/error/incomplete，`issues[]` 保留原因码与文件/字段。执行失败优先于评分，失败运行即使得到 100% 的 grading 也不进入通过率。`execution_status=unknown` 表示外部执行器未提供退出状态，不能宣称已验证执行成功。\n- summary 的 passed/failed/total 必须是非负整数、total>0、passed+failed=total。缺省 pass_rate 从计数推导；提供时必须为 [0,1] 有限数且与比值一致（允许两位小数舍入）。非空 expectations 必须是完整列表，布尔 passed 的计数与 summary 一致；summary-only 旧记录可读。\n- tokens/time/tool_calls 未知为 null，不能用 output_chars 估算 tokens。数值必须有限且非负；真实 0 保留。每项统计记录有效 n，n=0 的均值、标准差、最小、最大均为 null；Markdown 显示 N/A。\n- 通过率仅统计 completed；成本统计所有尝试中已知的观测值，含失败尝试，避免隐藏失败开销。`measurement_sources` 指明原始字段。\n- model/client 来自 metrics（缺省回退内嵌 execution_metrics）。保留 models/model_source/requested_model/tokens_source/tool_calls_source，区分客户端观测与请求参数。非空观测 models 集合优先于单个 model；多个已知模型时 model=null，不用 requested_model 伪装为单模型。空集合表示未记录的默认模型，保留未知。metadata 汇总模型/客户端集合及未知次数；仅当全部尝试明确记录同一模型时设置 executor_model，其他情况为 null。\n\n### 配对与增益边界\n\n- configuration 默认为 with_skill/without_skill；`--primary`/`--baseline` 显式指定角色，也识别 skill/treatment、baseline/control/no_skill 别名。未知角色不自动拿无关目录作对照。\n- 配对键为 **eval_id + run_number**。eval_id 优先来自场景的 eval_metadata.json，缺省用 eval 目录名；不同场景或不同重复编号不能配对。同一配置重复键标记歧义，不任选一条。\n- expected_pairs 是两配置已观察键的并集大小；paired_samples 只计两侧各有唯一 completed 记录且无已知 models 集合/client 差异的配对；coverage 为其比例。这里只覆盖已发现目录，**无法证明未创建目录的计划场景也已运行**。\n- 仅所有已观察键完整配对时输出通过率差值；缺失、失败、重号或已知模型/客户端不一致时 delta=null，并给 note，避免幸存样本制造增益。未记录模型/客户端的旧数据仍可描述性比较，但身份一致性未经确认。\n- 成本差值另要求所有配对都提供该指标；否则仅该项 delta=null。差值方向为 primary−baseline，沿用原字符串格式。均值与标准差是描述统计；一次配对不证明显著性或因果关系。\n- benchmark.md 展示每项 n、各配置 attempted/completed/errors/incomplete/coverage 和配对覆盖。分析时先检查完整性与模型身份，再解释差值及方差。\n\n## 工作区布局（约定）\n\n```\n<workspace>/iteration-N/\n└── eval-<descriptive-name>/\n    ├── with_skill/\n    │   └── run-1/\n    │       ├── transcript.md      # skill_scenario.py\n    │       ├── metrics.json       # skill_scenario.py（run 根，见上）\n    │       ├── timing.json\n    │       ├── outputs/\n    │       │   └── response.txt   # skill_scenario.py\n    │       └── grading.json       # 评分子代理\n    └── without_skill/\n        └── run-1/\n            └── ...                # 同结构\n```\n\n对比顺序：先检查尝试覆盖、配对完整性与模型/客户端身份，再解释可用的通过率和成本差值。高方差提示需要更多重复运行；不要仅凭标准差把原因定为 flaky。","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":251,"totalLines":251,"truncated":false}},"title":"Users\\Administrator\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\scenario-vtnlmomu\\.opencode\\skills\\skill-creator\\references\\benchmark-schema.md","time":{"start":1790677401811,"end":1790677401895}},"id":"prt_0ecb0c0b2001F2L6557XJ5LrRy","sessionID":"ses_f134f7d0fffeZt5oEc0aUxu205","messageID":"msg_0ecb0b72b0011oZG1wT4EqAWdb"}}

#!/usr/bin/env python3\n2: \"\"\"Aggregate benchmark run results into summary statistics (benchmark.json + benchmark.md).\n3: \n4: Port from Anthropic's official anthropics/skills skill-creator\n5: (aggregate_benchmark.py), kept client-agnostic (pure stdlib).\n6: \n7: Reads grading.json / timing.json / metrics.json from a workspace layout and produces:\n8:   - <dir>/benchmark.json  — machine-readable summary with mean/stddev/min/max + delta\n9:   - <dir>/benchmark.md    — human-readable table (pass rate / time / tokens)\n10: \n11: Usage:\n12:     python scripts/skill_benchmark.py <workspace>/iteration-N --skill-name <name> [--skill-path <path>] [--notes <notes.json>]\n13: \n14: Layouts supported:\n15:     <workspace>/iteration-N/\n16:     └── eval-<name>/\n17:         ├── with_skill/run-1/grading.json   (+ optional timing.json)\n18:         └── without_skill/run-1/grading.json\n19: \n20: --notes merges analyzer observations (a JSON array of strings produced by the\n21: analyzer subagent, see agents/skill_analyzer.md mode 2) into benchmark.json's notes.\n22: \"\"\"\n23: \n24: import argparse\n25: import json\n26: import math\n27: import statistics\n28: import sys\n29: from datetime import datetime, timezone\n30: from pathlib import Path\n31: \n32: SCRIPT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n33: \n34: \n35: def configure_utf8_output() -> None:\n36:     if sys.platform != \"win32\":\n37:         return\n38:     for stream_name in (\"stdout\", \"stderr\"):\n39:         stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)\n40:         if not stream:\n41:             continue\n42:         try:\n43:             stream.reconfigure(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"backslashreplace\")\n44:         except Exception:\n45:             pass\n46: \n47: \n48: def calculate_stats(values: list[float]) -> dict:\n49:     # Drop non-numeric / non-finite entries so NaN/Infinity never reach the JSON\n50:     # artifact (which the schema calls machine-readable).\n51:     clean: list[float] = []\n52:     for v in values:\n53:         if isinstance(v, bool):\n54:             continue\n55:         try:\n56:             f = float(v)\n57:         except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):\n58:             continue\n59:         if math.isfinite(f):\n60:             clean.append(f)\n61:     values = clean\n62:     if not values:\n63:         return {\"n\": 0, \"mean\": None, \"stddev\": None, \"min\": None, \"max\": None}\n64:     n = len(values)\n65:     mean = statistics.mean(values)\n66:     stddev = statistics.stdev(values) if n > 1 else 0.0\n67:     return {\n68:         \"n\": n,\n69:         \"mean\": round(mean, 4),\n70:         \"stddev\": round(stddev, 4),\n71:         \"min\": round(min(values), 4),\n72:         \"max\": round(max(values), 4),\n73:     }\n74: \n75: \n76: def _as_float(value, default=None):\n77:     \"\"\"Coerce a possibly-string/None/odd/non-finite field to a finite float.\"\"\"\n78:     if isinstance(value, bool):\n79:         return default\n80:     try:\n81:         x = float(value)\n82:     except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):\n83:         return default\n84:     # JSON accepts Infinity/NaN/1e400; rejecting them keeps benchmark.json valid\n85:     # strict JSON and stops inf/nan propagating into stats and int conversion.\n86:     return x if math.isfinite(x) else default\n87: \n88: \n89: def _as_int(value, default=None):\n90:     x = _as_float(value, default)\n91:     if x is None or x != int(x):\n92:         return default\n93:     try:\n94:         return int(x)\n95:     except (OverflowError, ValueError):\n96:         return default\n97: \n98: \n99: def _coerce_eval_id(value, default):\n100:     \"\"\"Only hashed-safe, JSON-safe scalars may become an eval_id (feeds set/sort).\"\"\"\n101:     if isinstance(value, bool):\n102:         return value\n103:     if isinstance(value, float):\n104:         return value if math.isfinite(value) else default\n105:     if isinstance(value, (str, int)):\n106:         return value\n107:     return default\n108: \n109: \n110: def _load_object(path: Path, issues: list[dict], required: bool = False) -> dict:\n111:     if not path.exists():\n112:         if required:\n113:             issues.append({\"code\": \"missing_artifact\", \"path\": path.name})\n114:         return {}\n115:     try:\n116:         data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8-sig\"))\n117:         if isinstance(data, dict):\n118:             return data\n119:     except (ValueError, OSError, UnicodeError):\n120:         pass\n121:     issues.append({\"code\": \"invalid_artifact\", \"path\": path.name})\n122:     return {}\n123: \n124: \n125: def _grading_result(grading: dict, issues: list[dict]) -> dict:\n126:     summary = grading.get(\"summary\")\n127:     if not isinstance(summary, dict):\n128:         issues.append({\"code\": \"invalid_grading\", \"detail\": \"missing summary object\"})\n129:         summary = {}\n130:     counts = {key: _as_int(summary.get(key)) for key in (\"passed\", \"failed\", \"total\")}\n131:     passed, failed, total = (counts[key] for key in (\"passed\", \"failed\", \"total\"))\n132:     valid_counts = (all(v is not None and v >= 0 for v in counts.values())\n133:                     and total > 0 and passed + failed == total)\n134:     rate = _as_float(summary.get(\"pass_rate\"))\n135:     if \"pass_rate\" not in summary and valid_counts:\n136:         rate = passed / total\n137:     valid_rate = (rate is not None and 0 <= rate <= 1\n138:                   and valid_counts and abs(rate - passed / total) <= 0.0051)\n139:     if not valid_counts or not valid_rate:\n140:         issues.append({\"code\": \"invalid_grading\", \"detail\": \"inconsistent counts or pass_rate\"})\n141:     expectations = grading.get(\"expectations\", [])\n142:     valid_expectations = isinstance(expectations, list) and all(\n143:         isinstance(e, dict) and isinstance(e.get(\"passed\"), bool)\n144:         and isinstance(e.get(\"text\"), str) and isinstance(e.get(\"evidence\"), str)\n145:         for e in expectations\n146:     )\n147:     if valid_expectations and expectations:\n148:         valid_expectations = (len(expectations) == total\n149:                               and sum(e[\"passed\"] for e in expectations) == passed)\n150:     if not valid_expectations:\n151:         issues.append({\"code\": \"invalid_grading\", \"detail\": \"expectations disagree with summary\"})\n152:     return {**counts, \"pass_rate\": rate if valid_rate and valid_expectations else None,\n153:             \"expectations\": [{k: e[k] for k in (\"text\", \"passed\", \"evidence\")}\n154:                              for e in expectations] if valid_expectations else []}\n155: \n156: \n157: def _measurement(sources: list[tuple[str, dict, str]], name: str,\n158:                  issues: list[dict], origins: dict, integer: bool = \n264: \n265: def _ordered_configs(results: dict, primary: str, baseline: str) -> list[str]:\n266:     \"\"\"Order config names as (primary, baseline, *rest) with graceful fallbacks.\n267: \n268:     Delta direction must not depend on directory-name sort order: with two\n269:     configs named `baseline`/`skill`, alphabetic order silently flips the sign of\n270:     the reported gain. Prefer the configured roles, then any known aliases, then\n271:     input order.\n272:     \"\"\"\n273:     aliases = {\n274:         \"with_skill\": [\"with_skill\", \"skill\", \"treatment\"],\n275:         \"without_skill\": [\"without_skill\", \"baseline\", \"control\", \"no_skill\"],\n276:     }\n277:     names = list(results.keys())\n278:     nameset = set(names)\n279: \n280:     def _resolve(role: str) -> str | None:\n281:         \"\"\"Resolve a role to an actual config name (exact first, then aliases).\"\"\"\n282:         if role in nameset:\n283:             return role\n284:         for alias in aliases.get(role, []):\n285:             if alias in nameset:\n286:                 return alias\n287:         return None\n288: \n289:     ordered: list[str] = []\n290:     # Resolve each role INDEPENDENTLY before ordering. Resolving them in one pass\n291:     # let a baseline alias land in slot 0 when the primary was absent by exact\n292:     # name (e.g. configs `skill`/`without_skill`: `without_skill` matched first,\n293:     # becoming the \"primary\"), silently flipping the delta sign.\n294:     for role in (primary, baseline):\n295:         resolved = _resolve(role)\n296:         if resolved and resolved not in ordered:\n297:             ordered.append(resolved)\n298:     for name in names:\n299:         if name not in ordered:\n300:             ordered.append(name)\n301:     return ordered\n302: \n303: \n304: def _resolve_role(results: dict, role: str) -> str | None:\n305:     aliases = {\"with_skill\": (\"skill\", \"treatment\"),\n306:                \"without_skill\": (\"baseline\", \"control\", \"no_skill\")}\n307:     return next((name for name in (role, *aliases.get(role, ())) if name in results), None)\n308: \n309: \n310: def aggregate_results(results: dict[str, list[dict]], primary: str = \"with_skill\",\n311:                       baseline: str = \"without_skill\") -> dict:\n312:     run_summary: dict = {}\n313:     for config in _ordered_configs(results, primary, baseline):\n314:         runs = results[config]\n315:         completed = [r for r in runs if r.get(\"status\") == \"completed\"]\n316:         run_summary[config] = {\n317:             \"runs\": len(runs), \"attempted\": len(runs), \"completed\": len(completed),\n318:             \"errors\": sum(r.get(\"status\") == \"error\" for r in runs),\n319:             \"incomplete\": sum(r.get(\"status\") == \"incomplete\" for r in runs),\n320:             \"coverage\": len(completed) / len(runs) if runs else 0.0,\n321:             \"pass_rate\": calculate_stats([r.get(\"pass_rate\") for r in completed]),\n322:             # Cost includes every observed attempt, including failed executions.\n323:             \"time_seconds\": calculate_stats([r.get(\"time_seconds\") for r in runs]),\n324:             \"tokens\": calculate_stats([r.get(\"tokens\") for r in runs]),\n325:         }\n326:     p_name, b_name = _resolve_role(results, primary), _resolve_role(results, baseline)\n327:     def indexed(name):\n328:         entries: dict[tuple, list] = {}\n329:         for run in results.get(name, []):\n330:             key = (json.dumps(run[\"eval_id\"], ensure_ascii=False), str(run[\"run_number\"]))\n331:             entries.setdefault(key, []).append(run)\n332:         return entries\n333:     p_runs, b_runs = indexed(p_name), indexed(b_name)\n334:     keys = p_runs.keys() | b_runs.keys()\n335:     paired = []\n336:     duplicate_keys, identity_mismatches = [], []\n337:     for key in sorted(keys):\n338:         left, right = p_runs.get(key, []), b_runs.get(key, [])\n339:         if len(left) > 1 or len(right) > 1:\n340:             duplicate_keys.append({\"eval_id\": json.loads(key[0]), \"run_number\": key[1]})\n341:         if len(left) != 1 or len(right) != 1:\n342:             continue\n343:         p, b = left[0], right[0]\n344:         if p.get(\"status\") != \"completed\" or b.get(\"status\") != \"completed\":\n345:             continue\n346:         mismatched = [field for field in (\"models\", \"client\")\n347:                       if p.get(field) and b.get(field) and p[field] != b[field]]\n348:         if mismatched:\n349:             identity_mismatches.append({\"eval_id\": p[\"eval_id\"], \"run_number\": p[\"run_number\"],\n350:                                         \"fields\": mismatched})\n351:             continue\n352:         paired.append((p, b))\n353:     complete = bool(keys) and p_name != b_name and len(paired) == len(keys)\n354:     delta = {\"primary\": p_name, \"baseline\": b_name,\n355:              \"expected_pairs\": len(keys), \"paired_samples\": len(paired),\n356:              \"coverage\": len(paired) / len(keys) if keys else 2), (\"time_seconds\", 1), (\"tokens\", 0)):\n360:         differences = [p[metric] - b[metric] for p, b in paired\n361:                        if p.get(metric) is not None and b.get(metric) is not None]\n362:         delta[\"metric_pairs\"][metric] = len(differences)\n363:         delta[metric] = (f\"{statistics.mean(differences):+.{precision}f}\"\n364:                          if complete and len(differences) == len(keys) else None)\n365:     if not complete:\n366:         delta[\"note\"] = (\"delta unavailable: every observed eval_id + run_number needs one completed \"\n367: {metadata.get('unknown_client_runs', 'N/A')}\",\n430:         f\"**Date**: {metadata['timestamp']}\",\n431:         f\"**Evals**: {', '.join(map(str, metadata['evals_run']))} (up to (e.g. \"+0.60\"); render it in the same\n442:         # percent units as the columns so \"+0.60\" does not read as +0.6%.\n443:         if value is None:\n444:             return \"N/A\"\n445: \"\n460:                 f\"{(stat.get('stddev') or 0) * scale:.{precision}f}{unit} (n={stat.get('n', 'N/A')})\")\n461:     for metric, label, precision, scale, unit in ((\"pass_rate\", \"Pass Rate\", benchmark.get(\"notes\"):\n481:         lines.extend([\"\", \"## Notes\", \"\"])\n482:         lines.extend(f\"- {n}\" for n in benchmark[\"notes\"])\n483:     return \"\\n\".join(lines) + json.loads(notes_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8-sig\"))\n489:     if isinstance(data, dict):\n490:         if \"notes\" not in data or not isinstance(data[\"notes\"], list):\n491:             raise ValueError(\"object-shaped notes file must contain a 'notes' array\")\n492:         data = data[\"notes\"]\n493:     if not isinstance(data, list) or not all(isinstance(n, str) for n in data):\n494:         raise ValueError(\"notes file must be a JSON array of strings\")\n495:     return data\n496: \n497: \n498: def main() -> int:\n499:     configure_utf8_output()\n500:     parser = directory\")\n502:     parser.add_argument(\"--skill-name\", default=\"\", help=\"Name of the skill being benchmarked\")\n503:     parser.add_argument(\"--skill-path\", default=\"\", help=\"Path to print(f\"Directory not found: {args.benchmark_dir}\")\n516:         return 1\n517:     if not args.benchmark_dir.is_dir():\n518:         print(f\"Not a directory: {args.benchmark_dir}\")\n519:         return 1\n520: \n521:     benchmark = generate_benchmark(args.benchmark_dir, args.skill_name, args.skill_path,\n522:                                    args.primary, args.baseline)\n523:     if lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Aggregate benchmark run results into summary statistics (benchmark.json + benchmark.md).\n\nPort from Anthropic's official kept client-agnostic (pure stdlib).\n\nReads grading.json / timing.json / metrics.json from a workspace layout and produces:\n  - <dir>/benchmark.json  — machine-readable summary with
